# Skills · P · part 57

887 entries, Plan Under Uncertainty to plan-creation. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **Plan Under Uncertainty**: Builds plans that survive changing conditions by identifying assumptions, creating decision points, and keeping options open. Helps teams plan without pretending to know the future. Trigger on: uncertain requirements, market shifts, 'what if', contingency planning, strategic planning, scenario analysis, decision frameworks, 'if X happens'. ([heyharmony/skills](https://github.com/heyharmony/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/plan-under-uncertainty) · ★1)
